== Linux fzax default
size 640x480
run /c/dolphin/dolphin-emu-nogui -u /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin -p x11 -e /lib/fzax/F-Zero AX (Triforce) (Monster) [JAP][BHA6].iso
cwd /d/runtimes/fzax
env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/c/dolphin/lib
env ENABLE_GAMESCOPE_WSI=0
gamescope -w 640 -h 480 -W 3840 -H 2160 -r 60 -F fsr --sharpness 5 -f
feed Dolphin pipe /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Pipes/launcher
file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/Dolphin.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Core]
SIDevice0 = 11
SIDevice1 = 0
SIDevice2 = 0
SIDevice3 = 0
SerialPort1 = 6
SkipIPL = True
GFXBackend = Vulkan
EnableCheats = True
[Display]
Fullscreen = True
RenderToMain = False
KeepWindowOnTop = True
[Interface]
UsePanicHandlers = False
ConfirmStop = False
OnScreenDisplayMessages = True
PauseOnFocusLost = False
ShowActiveTitle = False
[Analytics]
Enabled = False
PermissionAsked = True
[AutoUpdate]
UpdateTrack = 
[NetPlay]
UseUPNP = False

file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/GFX.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Hardware]
VSync = True
[Settings]
InternalResolution = 1
ShaderCompilationMode = 0
WaitForShadersBeforeStarting = True
ShowFPS = False

file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/GameSettings/GHAE6E.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Gecko]
$Bypass CyCraft (no motion seat)
0043137B 00000086
[Gecko_Enabled]
$Bypass CyCraft (no motion seat)

file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/GCPadNew.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[GCPad1]
Device = Pipe/0/launcher
Main Stick/Gate Size = 100.0
Main Stick/Dead Zone = 0.0
Triggers/Threshold = 90.0
Options/Always Connected = True
Buttons/A = `Button A` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:z`
Buttons/B = `Button B` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:x`
D-Pad/Right = `Button D_RIGHT` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:x`
Buttons/X = `Button X` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:a`
Buttons/Y = `Button Y` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:s`
Buttons/Start = `Button START` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Return`
Triforce/Coin = `Button Z` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:5`
Triforce/Test = `Button L` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:9`
Triforce/Service = `Button R` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:0`
Main Stick/Left = `Axis MAIN X -` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Left`
Main Stick/Right = `Axis MAIN X +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Right`
Main Stick/Up = `Axis MAIN Y -` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:w`
Main Stick/Down = `Axis MAIN Y +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:q`
Triggers/R-Analog = `Axis R +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Up`
Triggers/L-Analog = `Axis L +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Down`
[GCPad2]
Device = 
[GCPad3]
Device = 
[GCPad4]
Device = 

== Linux fzax default segaboot
size 640x480
run /c/dolphin/dolphin-emu-nogui -u /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin -p x11 -e /lib/fzax/F-Zero AX (Triforce) (Monster) [JAP][BHA6].iso
cwd /d/runtimes/fzax
env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/c/dolphin/lib
env ENABLE_GAMESCOPE_WSI=0
gamescope -w 640 -h 480 -W 3840 -H 2160 -r 60 -F fsr --sharpness 5 -f
feed Dolphin pipe /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Pipes/launcher
link /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Triforce/segaboot.gcm -> /bios/segaboot.gcm
file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/Dolphin.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Core]
SIDevice0 = 11
SIDevice1 = 0

ShowActiveTitle = False
[Analytics]
Enabled = False

== Linux fzax default headless
size 640x480
run /c/dolphin/dolphin-emu-nogui -u /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin -p x11 -e /lib/fzax/F-Zero AX (Triforce) (Monster) [JAP][BHA6].iso
cwd /d/runtimes/fzax
env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/c/dolphin/lib
env ENABLE_GAMESCOPE_WSI=0
gamescope -w 640 -h 480 -W 3840 -H 2160 -r 60 -F linear --backend headless
feed Dolphin pipe /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Pipes/launcher

== Linux fzax custom
size 1920x1440
run /c/dolphin/dolphin-emu-nogui -u /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin -p x11 -e /lib/fzax/F-Zero AX (Triforce) (Monster) [JAP][BHA6].iso
cwd /d/runtimes/fzax
env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/c/dolphin/lib
env ENABLE_GAMESCOPE_WSI=0
gamescope -w 1920 -h 1440 -W 2560 -H 1440 -r 60 -F nis --sharpness 6 --reshade-effect crt-lottes.fx
gamescope HOME /d/runtimes/fzax/gamescope-home
feed Dolphin pipe /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Pipes/launcher
link /d/runtimes/fzax/gamescope-home/.local/share/gamescope/reshade/Shaders/crt-lottes.fx -> /c/shaders/crt-lottes.fx
file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/Dolphin.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Core]
SIDevice0 = 11
SIDevice1 = 0
SIDevice2 = 0
SIDevice3 = 0
SerialPort1 = 6
SkipIPL = True
GFXBackend = Vulkan
EnableCheats = True
[Display]
Fullscreen = True
RenderToMain = False
KeepWindowOnTop = True
[Interface]
UsePanicHandlers = False
ConfirmStop = False
OnScreenDisplayMessages = True
PauseOnFocusLost = False
ShowActiveTitle = False
[Analytics]
Enabled = False
PermissionAsked = True
[AutoUpdate]
UpdateTrack = 
[NetPlay]
UseUPNP = False

file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/GFX.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Hardware]
VSync = True
[Settings]
InternalResolution = 3
ShaderCompilationMode = 0

ShowFPS = False

== Linux fzax custom headless segaboot
size 1920x1440
run /c/dolphin/dolphin-emu-nogui -u /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin -p x11 -e /lib/fzax/F-Zero AX (Triforce) (Monster) [JAP][BHA6].iso
cwd /d/runtimes/fzax
env LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/c/dolphin/lib
env ENABLE_GAMESCOPE_WSI=0
gamescope -w 1920 -h 1440 -W 2560 -H 1440 -r 60 -F linear --reshade-effect crt-lottes.fx --backend headless
gamescope HOME /d/runtimes/fzax/gamescope-home
feed Dolphin pipe /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Pipes/launcher
link /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Triforce/segaboot.gcm -> /bios/segaboot.gcm
link /d/runtimes/fzax/gamescope-home/.local/share/gamescope/reshade/Shaders/crt-lottes.fx -> /c/shaders/crt-lottes.fx
file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/Dolphin.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Core]
SIDevice0 = 11
SIDevice1 = 0
SIDevice2 = 0
SIDevice3 = 0
SerialPort1 = 6
SkipIPL = True
GFXBackend = Vulkan
EnableCheats = True
[Display]
Fullscreen = True
RenderToMain = False
KeepWindowOnTop = True
[Interface]
UsePanicHandlers = False
ConfirmStop = False
OnScreenDisplayMessages = True
PauseOnFocusLost = False
ShowActiveTitle = False
[Analytics]
Enabled = False
PermissionAsked = True
[AutoUpdate]
UpdateTrack = 
[NetPlay]
UseUPNP = False

file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/GFX.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Hardware]
VSync = True
[Settings]
InternalResolution = 3
ShaderCompilationMode = 0
WaitForShadersBeforeStarting = True
ShowFPS = False

file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/GameSettings/GHAE6E.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[Gecko]
$Bypass CyCraft (no motion seat)
0043137B 00000086
[Gecko_Enabled]
$Bypass CyCraft (no motion seat)

file /d/runtimes/fzax/dolphin/Config/GCPadNew.ini
# Written by the launcher before every start.
[GCPad1]
Device = Pipe/0/launcher
Main Stick/Gate Size = 100.0
Main Stick/Dead Zone = 0.0
Triggers/Threshold = 90.0
Options/Always Connected = True
Buttons/A = `Button A` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:z`
Buttons/B = `Button B` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:x`
D-Pad/Right = `Button D_RIGHT` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:x`
Buttons/X = `Button X` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:a`
Buttons/Y = `Button Y` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:s`
Buttons/Start = `Button START` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Return`
Triforce/Coin = `Button Z` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:5`
Triforce/Test = `Button L` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:9`
Triforce/Service = `Button R` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:0`
Main Stick/Left = `Axis MAIN X -` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Left`
Main Stick/Right = `Axis MAIN X +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Right`
Main Stick/Up = `Axis MAIN Y -` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:w`
Main Stick/Down = `Axis MAIN Y +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:q`
Triggers/R-Analog = `Axis R +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Up`
Triggers/L-Analog = `Axis L +` | `XInput2/0/Virtual core pointer:Down`
[GCPad2]
Device = 
[GCPad3]

[GCPad4]

[GCPad1]
Device = XInput/0/Gamepad
Main Stick/Gate Size = 100.0
Main Stick/Dead Zone = 0.0
Triggers/Threshold = 90.0
Options/Always Connected = True
Buttons/A = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Button A` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:Z`
Buttons/B = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Button B` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:X`
D-Pad/Right = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Button B` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:X`
Buttons/X = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Shoulder L` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:A`
Buttons/Y = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Shoulder R` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:S`
Buttons/Start = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Start` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:RETURN`
Triforce/Coin = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Back` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:5`
Triforce/Test = `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:9`
Triforce/Service = `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:0`
Main Stick/Left = `SDL/0/Test Wheel:Axis 0-` | `XInput/0/Gamepad:Left X-` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:LEFT`
Main Stick/Right = `SDL/0/Test Wheel:Axis 0+` | `XInput/0/Gamepad:Left X+` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:RIGHT`
Main Stick/Up = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Left Y+` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:W`
Main Stick/Down = `XInput/0/Gamepad:Left Y-` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:Q`
Triggers/R-Analog = `SDL/0/Test Wheel:Axis 1+` | `XInput/0/Gamepad:Trigger R` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:UP`
Triggers/L-Analog = `SDL/0/Test Wheel:Axis 2-` | `XInput/0/Gamepad:Trigger L` | `DInput/0/Keyboard Mouse:DOWN`
